\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 16. Reusable Handoff Pack for Next Stage\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 COMMERCIAL-OUTPUT-SPEC is complete. The Commercial branch has now been converted from approved revised baseline wording into a concrete output-specificity standard, preserved non-regression test map, adversarial scenario pack and display-layer verification checklist. No live Commercial reports, JSONs, screenshots or comparison-page examples were supplied, so this is not live-output QA and does not verify production rendering. It is a synthetic/adversarial map designed to support implementation planning and later live Commercial output QA.\
The output-specificity rule set contains COMMERCIAL-OS-R01\'96R21. The most important rules are Commercial distinctiveness, acting-as-presence/naturalism semantics, generic praise suppression, tone/copy/product grounding, no-brief grounding, task-specific camera and eyeline handling, assessability as a precondition, audio reliability, simple capture / anti-polish, process-only reader/admin/deadline boundaries, live-room-only overclaim boundaries, accessibility-safe non-deficit language, anti-marketability / anti-appearance exclusion, professional_presentation boundaries and display-layer verification.\
The test set maps COMMERCIAL-AUDIT-T01\'96T30 into COMMERCIAL-OS-T01\'96T30, with one additional safety test COMMERCIAL-OS-T31 for nudity / stunt / privacy / consent boundaries. Highest-priority tests are direct-to-camera Commercial, off-camera-reader Commercial, no-brief Commercial, supplied brand/product/copy, generic \'93natural/confident\'94 wording, poor lighting/framing, poor audio, assessable home capture, high polish with weak performance, paid/basic reader, response-to-direction overclaim, callback-readiness overclaim, no-brief brand/product/audience invention, marketability/bookability/look language, access-adapted context, speech/accent/gender-diverse voice safety, production polish in professional_presentation and timestamp underproduction.\
Residual limitations are COMMERCIAL-OS-I01\'96I15. The major limitations are no live outputs, unverified frontend/comparison/rendering behaviour, under-evidenced UGC/social, presenter-led and corporate/industrial subtypes, conditional-only voiceover support, partly inferential anti-marketability evidence and broad access detail for accent, speech difference and gender-diverse voice.\
Evidence basis: COMMERCIAL-S001\'96S030, COMMERCIAL-B1-F01\'96F09, COMMERCIAL-B2-F01\'96F09, COMMERCIAL-B3-F01\'96F10, COMMERCIAL-B4-F01\'96F06, COMMERCIAL-SYN-F01\'96F24, COMMERCIAL-AUDIT-I01\'96I17, COMMERCIAL-AUDIT-R01\'96R21, COMMERCIAL-REV-C01\'96C21, COMMERCIAL-REV-S01\'96S21, COMMERCIAL-FINAL-D01 and COMMERCIAL-FINAL-D02.\
What was testable: revised baseline wording, rule derivation, claim-scope restrictions, generic phrase suppression, adversarial test logic and preserved baseline constraints.\
What was not testable: live Commercial report quality, Commercial JSON/report-object behaviour, frontend labels, comparison-page labels, renderer/export timestamp parity and score-stability behaviour.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0
